\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-2}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-1}q}\right\}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-2}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}\bar{q}^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-2}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{2\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}\bar{q}^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-2}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-1}\bar{q}}\right\}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\},

\displaystyle\hat{D}^{d}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}-1}q^{\eta_{k}+\eta_{k+1}}}+\frac{(2q^{-1}-1)\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}-1}q^{\eta_{k}+1}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k+1}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k+1}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-2}q^{3}}+\frac{(2q^{-1}-1)\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-2}q^{2}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k+1}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k+1}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\},

\displaystyle\hat{E}^{d}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}-1}\bar{q}^{\eta_{k}+\eta_{k+1}}}+\frac{(2\bar{q}^{-1}-1)\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}-1}\bar{q}^{\eta_{k}+1}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k+1}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k+1}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-2}\bar{q}^{3}}+\frac{(2\bar{q}^{-1}-1)\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-2}\bar{q}^{2}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k+1}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k+1}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\},

\displaystyle\hat{\bar{F}}^{d}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{-(\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N})}{2^{-\eta_{k}-1}q^{\eta_{k}}\bar{q}^{\eta_{k+1}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k+1}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k+1}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{-(\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N})}{2^{-2}q\bar{q}^{2}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k+1}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k+1}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{2\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-1}q}\right\}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=2}^{K-2}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}\bar{q}^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\},

\displaystyle\hat{\bar{G}}^{d}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{-(\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N})}{2^{-\eta_{k}-1}q^{\eta_{k+1}}\bar{q}^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k+1}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k+1}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{-(\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N})}{2^{-2}q^{2}\bar{q}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k+1}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k+1}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}\bar{q}^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{2\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}\bar{q}^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-1}\bar{q}}\right\}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=2}^{K-2}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\},

\displaystyle\hat{A}^{s}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K-2}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{3(\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N})}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{1,z}^{2\eta_{k}}}+\frac{(4q_{1,z}^{-2}-1)\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{1,z}^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-2}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{2(\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N})}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{1,z}^{\eta_{k}+1}}+\frac{(4q_{1,z}^{-1}-2)\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{1,z}^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-2}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-1}q_{1,z}^{2}}+\frac{(2q_{1,z}^{-1}-1)\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-1}q_{1,z}}\right\}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\},

\displaystyle\hat{B}^{s}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K-2}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{3(\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N})}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{2,z}^{2\eta_{k}}}+\frac{(4q_{2,z}^{-2}-1)\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{2,z}^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-2}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{2(\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N})}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{2,z}^{\eta_{k}+1}}+\frac{(4q_{2,z}^{-1}-2)\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{2,z}^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-2}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-1}q_{2,z}^{2}}+\frac{(2q_{2,z}^{-1}-1)\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-1}q_{2,z}}\right\}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\},

\displaystyle\hat{C}^{s}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K-2}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{1,z}^{2\eta_{k}}}+\frac{\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{1,z}^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-2}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{2(\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N})}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{1,z}^{\eta_{k}+1}}+\frac{2\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{1,z}^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-2}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-1}q_{1,z}^{2}}+\frac{\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-1}q_{1,z}}\right\}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\},

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-2}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{2,z}^{2\eta_{k}}}+\frac{\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{2,z}^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-2}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{2(\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N})}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{2,z}^{\eta_{k}+1}}+\frac{2\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{2,z}^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-2}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-1}q_{2,z}^{2}}+\frac{\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-1}q_{2,z}}\right\}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\},

\displaystyle\hat{D}^{s}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}-1}q_{1,z}^{\eta_{k}+\eta_{k+1}}}+\frac{(2q_{1,z}^{-1}-1)\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}-1}q_{1,z}^{\eta_{k}+1}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k+1}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k+1}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-2}q_{1,z}^{3}}+\frac{(2q_{1,z}^{-1}-1)\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-2}q_{1,z}^{2}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k+1}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k+1}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\},

\displaystyle\hat{E}^{s}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}-1}q_{2,z}^{\eta_{k}+\eta_{k+1}}}+\frac{(2q_{2,z}^{-1}-1)\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}-1}q_{2,z}^{\eta_{k}+1}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k+1}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k+1}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-2}q_{2,z}^{3}}+\frac{(2q_{2,z}^{-1}-1)\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-2}q_{2,z}^{2}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k+1}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k+1}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\},

\displaystyle\hat{\bar{F}}^{s}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{1,z}^{2\eta_{k}}}+\frac{\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{1,z}^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{2(\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N})}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{1,z}^{\eta_{k}+1}}+\frac{2\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{1,z}^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-1}q_{1,z}^{2}}+\frac{\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-1}q_{1,z}}\right\}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=2}^{K-2}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{2,z}^{2\eta_{k}}}+\frac{\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{2,z}^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\},

\displaystyle\hat{\bar{G}}^{s}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{2,z}^{2\eta_{k}}}+\frac{\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{2,z}^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{2(\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N})}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{2,z}^{\eta_{k}+1}}+\frac{2\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{2,z}^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\check{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K-3}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-1}q_{2,z}^{2}}+\frac{\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-1}q_{2,z}}\right\}\{\Delta{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=2}^{K-2}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{\top}\left\{\frac{\mathbf{J}_{N}-\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{1,z}^{2\eta_{k}}}+\frac{\mathbf{I}_{N}}{2^{-\eta_{k}}q_{1,z}^{\eta_{k}}}\right\}\{\check{\mathbf{Y}}_{k}\circ\tilde{\mathbf{I}}_{k}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}.

B
Exact optimal
r_{q_{1}}

For fixed
N
and
r_{q_{1}}+r_{q_{2}}\neq 1
, the exact optimal
r_{q_{1}}
may vary across different scenarios.
Figure

S1
shows the optimal
r_{q_{1}}
under different objective functions (combined risks), different population sizes
N
and different combinations of
\mathcal{J}_{j}
(including
\mathcal{J}_{1}:\mathcal{J}_{2}=2:1
,
\mathcal{J}_{1}:\mathcal{J}_{2}:\mathcal{J}_{3}=3:2:1
and
\mathcal{J}_{1}:\mathcal{J}_{2}:\mathcal{J}_{3}:\mathcal{J}_{4}=4:3:2:1
) when
q_{1}=0.8
and
q_{2}=0.5
. They all converge towards 0.5 as
N
tends to infinity.

Figure S1
:
Optimal
r_{q_{1}}
under different
N
and combinations of
\mathcal{J}_{j}
.

C
Proof of Theorem
1

Recall that
\mathbb{T}=\{t_{0},\ldots,t_{L}\}
is the set of decision points,
r_{q_{1}}
and
r_{q_{2}}
are the probabilities of choosing the treated probability from
q_{1}
and
q_{2}
,
\mathbb{Q}=\{Q_{t_{0}},Q_{t_{1}},\ldots,Q_{t_{L}}\}
is the set of treated probabilities,
\mathbb{Y}=\{Y_{i,t}(\mathbf{q}_{(t-p):t},\mathbf{z}_{i,(t-p):t})\mid i\in[N],\ t\in[T],\ \mathbf{q}_{(t-p):t}\in\{q_{1},q_{2}\}^{p+1},\ \mathbf{z}_{i,(t-p):t}\in\{0,1\}^{p+1}\}
is the set of all potential outcomes.

We first introduce the following notation:

1.

\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}(t)=\max\{j\mid j\in\mathbb{T},j\leq t\}
: This represents the maximum index
j
from the set of randomization decision points
\mathbb{T}
such that
j
is less than or equal to
t
. In simpler terms, it denotes the randomization time point that determines the treatment assignment at time
t
.

2.

\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}^{p}(t)=\{j\mid\exists i\in\{t-p,\ldots,t\},\text{ such that }j=\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}(i)\}
: This set includes all indices
j
for which there exists some
i
in the range
{t-p,\ldots,t}
such that
j=\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}(i)
. In other words, it consists of the randomization time points in
\mathbb{T}
that collectively determine the treatment assignments for the time periods
{t-p,\ldots,t}
. It is worth noting that
\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}^{p}(t)
is not empty for all
t
.

3.

J_{t}=|\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}^{p}(t)|
: This represents the number of elements in
\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}^{p}(t)
, denoting how many different points collectively determine the treatment assignment for the time period
t
. It is always greater than or equal to 1 and less than or equal to
p+1
.

4.

O_{\mathbb{T}}(t,t^{\prime})=\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}^{p}(t)\cap\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}^{p}(t^{\prime})
: This set includes the common randomization points between the time periods
{t-p,\ldots,t}
and
{t^{\prime}-p,\ldots,t^{\prime}}
. In other words, it is the set of overlapping randomization points that affect both of these time periods.

5.

J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=|O_{\mathbb{T}}(t,t^{\prime})|
: This represents the number of overlapping randomization points between the time periods
{t-p,\ldots,t}
and
{t^{\prime}-p,\ldots,t^{\prime}}
that determine treatment assignments. It is always less than or equal to both
J_{t}
and
J_{t^{\prime}}
.

Recall that the objective function is given by

\mathcal{L}(\psi_{d},\psi_{s})=\psi_{d}\{\mathrm{risk}^{d}(q_{1})+\mathrm{risk}^{d}(q_{2})\}+\psi_{s}\{\mathrm{risk}^{s}(1)+\mathrm{risk}^{s}(0)\}=\psi_{d}\mathcal{L}(1,0)+\psi_{s}\mathcal{L}(0,1).

This expression represents the weighted sum of the direct effect risk and the spillover effect risk. Here,
\psi_{d}
and
\psi_{s}
are the weights assigned to the direct and spillover risks, respectively. As a result, our primary goal is to obtain the theoretical results for
\mathcal{L}(1,0)
and
\mathcal{L}(0,1)
; subsequently, we combine these results to derive the results for
\mathcal{L}(\psi_{d},\psi_{s})
.

Recall that
q_{1,z}=zq_{1}+(1-z)(1-q_{1})
and
q_{2,z}=zq_{2}+(1-z)(1-q_{2})
. Given
r_{q_{1}},r_{q_{2}}
and
\mathbb{T}
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{pr}_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})=r_{q_{1}}^{J_{t}}q_{1,z}^{J_{t}},\quad\mathrm{pr}_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})=r_{q_{2}}^{J_{t}}q_{2,z}^{J_{t}},\quad z=0,1.

(S1)

Define

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},q)

\displaystyle=Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\left\{\frac{I_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})}{r_{q}^{J_{t}}q^{J_{t}}}-1\right\}-Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\left\{\frac{I_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})}{r_{q}^{J_{t}}\bar{q}^{J_{t}}}-1\right\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},z)

\displaystyle=Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\left\{\frac{I_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})}{r_{q_{1}}^{J_{t}}q_{1,z}^{J_{t}}}-1\right\}-Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\left\{\frac{I_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})}{r_{q_{2}}^{J_{t}}q_{2,z}^{J_{t}}}-1\right\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},q),\quad\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{S}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},z).

Then

\displaystyle\hat{\tau}^{d}(q)-\tau^{d}(q)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T-p}\sum_{t=p+1}^{T}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},q)=\frac{1}{T-p}\sum_{t=p+1}^{T}\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q),

\displaystyle\hat{\tau}^{s}(z)-\tau^{s}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T-p}\sum_{t=p+1}^{T}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{S}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},z)=\frac{1}{T-p}\sum_{t=p+1}^{T}\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z).

Note that
\hat{\tau}^{d}(q)
and
\hat{\tau}^{s}(z)
depend on
r_{q_{1}},r_{q_{2}}
,
\mathbb{T}
, and
\mathbb{Y}
. For simplicity, we will ignore these dependencies unless it leads to confusion.

Lemma S1

(Properties of
\mathbf{D}_{t}
and
\mathbf{S}_{t}
)
.

Under Assumptions
1
–
4
,
\mathbf{D}_{t}
and
\mathbf{S}_{t}
satisfy

(1.1)
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\}=0
;

(1.2) for
q=q_{1},q_{2}
,

\displaystyle N^{2}E\left\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(r_{q}^{-J_{t}}-1)\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})-\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\right\}^{2}+2r_{q}^{-J_{t}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\right\}

\displaystyle+r_{q}^{-J_{t}}(q^{-J_{t}}-1)\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\right\}^{2}+r_{q}^{-J_{t}}(\bar{q}^{-J_{t}}-1)\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\right\}^{2};

(1.3) for
q=q_{1},q_{2}
and
t\neq t^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle N^{2}E\left\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(r_{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})-\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\right\}\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})-\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\right\}

\displaystyle+r_{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\right\}+r_{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\right\}

\displaystyle+r_{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}(q^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\right\}

\displaystyle+r_{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}(\bar{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\right\};

(2.1)
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\}=0
;

(2.2) for
z=0,1
,

\displaystyle N^{2}E\left\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(r_{q_{1}}^{-J_{t}}-1)\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}^{2}+(r_{q_{2}}^{-J_{t}}-1)\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}

\displaystyle+r_{q_{1}}^{-J_{t}}(q_{1,z}^{-J_{t}}-1)\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}^{2}+r_{q_{2}}^{-J_{t}}(q_{2,z}^{-J_{t}}-1)\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}^{2};

(2.3) for
z=0,1
and
t\neq t^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle N^{2}E\left\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(r_{q_{1}}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}

\displaystyle+(r_{q_{2}}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}

\displaystyle+\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}+\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}

\displaystyle+r_{q_{1}}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}(q_{1,z}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}

\displaystyle+r_{q_{2}}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}(q_{2,z}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}.

Proof of Lemma
S1
.

(1.1) By
Equation

S1
, it is easy to see that
E\{\mathbf{D}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},q)\}=0
, which further implies that
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\}=0
.

(1.2) For
i\neq j
,

\displaystyle E\{\mathbf{D}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},q)\}^{2}=

\displaystyle(q^{-J_{t}}r_{q}^{-J_{t}}-1)\{Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\}^{2}+(\bar{q}^{-J_{t}}r_{q}^{-J_{t}}-1)\{Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}^{2}

\displaystyle+2Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0}),

\displaystyle E\{\mathbf{D}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{j,t}(\mathbb{T},q)\}=

\displaystyle(r_{q}^{-J_{t}}-1)\{Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})-Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}\{Y_{j,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})-Y_{j,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}.

The result follows from

\displaystyle N^{2}E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\}^{2}=\sum_{i=1}^{N}E\{\mathbf{D}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},q)\}^{2}+\sum_{i\neq j}E\{\mathbf{D}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{j,t}(\mathbb{T},q)\}.

(1.3) For
t\neq t^{\prime}
and
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}\neq 0
,

\displaystyle E\{\mathbf{D}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{i,t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}=

\displaystyle(q^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}r_{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})

\displaystyle+(\bar{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}r_{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})

\displaystyle+Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})+Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1}),

\displaystyle E\{\mathbf{D}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{j,t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}=

\displaystyle(r_{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)\{Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})-Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}\{Y_{j,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})-Y_{j,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\}.

The result follows from

\displaystyle N^{2}E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}=\sum_{i=1}^{N}E\{\mathbf{D}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{i,t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}+\sum_{i\neq j}E\{\mathbf{D}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{j,t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}.

(2.1) By
Equation

S1
, it is easy to see that
E\{\mathbf{S}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},z)\}=0
, which further implies that
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\}=0
,
z=0,1
.

(2.2) For
i\neq j
,

\displaystyle E\{\mathbf{S}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},z)\}^{2}=

\displaystyle(q_{1,z}^{-J_{t}}r_{q_{1}}^{-J_{t}}-1)\{Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{2}+(q_{2,z}^{-J_{t}}r_{q_{2}}^{-J_{t}}-1)\{Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\}^{2}

\displaystyle+2Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1}),

\displaystyle E\{\mathbf{S}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{j,t}(\mathbb{T},z)\}=

\displaystyle(r_{q_{1}}^{-J_{t}}-1)Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})Y_{j,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})+(r_{q_{2}}^{-J_{t}}-1)Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})Y_{j,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})

\displaystyle+Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})Y_{j,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})+Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})Y_{j,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1}).

The result follows from

\displaystyle N^{2}E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\}^{2}=\sum_{i=1}^{N}E\{\mathbf{S}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},z)\}^{2}+\sum_{i\neq j}E\{\mathbf{S}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{j,t}(\mathbb{T},z)\}.

(2.3) For
t\neq t^{\prime}
and
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}\neq 0
,

\displaystyle E\{\mathbf{S}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{i,t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}=

\displaystyle(q_{1,z}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}r_{q_{1}}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})

\displaystyle+(q_{2,z}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}r_{q_{2}}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})

\displaystyle+Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})+Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1}),

\displaystyle E\{\mathbf{S}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{j,t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}=

\displaystyle(r_{q_{1}}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})Y_{j,t^{\prime}}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})

\displaystyle+(r_{q_{2}}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})Y_{j,t^{\prime}}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})

\displaystyle+Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})Y_{j,t^{\prime}}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})+Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})Y_{j,t^{\prime}}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1}).

The result follows from

\displaystyle N^{2}E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}=\sum_{i=1}^{N}E\{\mathbf{S}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{i,t^{\prime}}(z)\}+\sum_{i\neq j}E\{\mathbf{S}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{j,t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}.

∎

Recall that
B
is the bound of potential outcomes as stated in
Assumption

4
.

Lemma S2
.

Under Assumptions
1
–
4
, given
\mathbb{T}^{*}
,
r_{q_{1}}
and
r_{q_{2}}
, we have

(1) when
N\geq(1-\max\{r_{q_{1}},r_{q_{2}}\})^{-1}
,
\mathop{\arg\max}_{\mathbb{Y}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathcal{L}(1,0)=\{Y_{i,t}(\mathbf{q}_{(t-p):t},\mathbf{z}_{i,(t-p):t})\mid Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=C_{1},Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=-C_{1},Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=C_{2},Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=-C_{2}\}
with
C_{1}=\pm B
,
C_{2}=\pm B
; when
N\leq(1-\min\{r_{q_{1}}^{p+1},r_{q_{2}}^{p+1}\})^{-1}
,
\mathop{\arg\max}_{\mathbb{Y}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathcal{L}(1,0)=\{Y_{i,t}(\mathbf{q}_{(t-p):t},\mathbf{z}_{i,(t-p):t})\mid Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=C_{1},Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=C_{1},Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=C_{2},Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=C_{2}\}
with
C_{1}=\pm B
and
C_{2}=\pm B
;

(2)
\mathop{\arg\max}_{\mathbb{Y}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathcal{L}(0,1)=\{Y_{i,t}(\mathbf{q}_{(t-p):t},\mathbf{z}_{i,(t-p):t})\mid Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=C_{3},Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=C_{4},Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=C_{3},Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=C_{4}\}
with
C_{3}=\pm B
and
C_{4}=\pm B
;

(3) for any
\psi_{d}>0
and
\psi_{s}>0
, when
N\geq(1-\max\{r_{q_{1}},r_{q_{0}}\})^{-1}
,
\mathop{\arg\max}_{\mathbb{Y}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathcal{L}(\psi_{d},\psi_{s})=\{Y_{i,t}(\mathbf{q}_{(t-p):t},\mathbf{z}_{i,(t-p):t})\mid Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=C,Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=-C,Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=C,Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=-C\}
with
C=\pm B
; when
N\leq(1-\min\{r_{q_{1}}^{p+1},r_{q_{0}}^{p+1}\})^{-1}
,
\mathop{\arg\max}_{\mathbb{Y}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathcal{L}(\psi_{d},\psi_{s})=\{Y_{i,t}(\mathbf{q}_{(t-p):t},\mathbf{z}_{i,(t-p):t})\mid Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=C,Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=C,Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=C,Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=C\}
with
C=\pm B
.

Proof of Lemma
S2
.

First of all, recall that

\displaystyle\mathrm{risk}^{d}(q)

\displaystyle=E\{\hat{\tau}^{d}(q)-\tau^{d}(q)\}^{2}=\frac{1}{(T-p)^{2}}\left[\sum_{t=p+1}^{T}E\left\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\right\}^{2}+\sum_{t\neq t^{\prime}}E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}\right],

\displaystyle\mathrm{risk}^{s}(z)

\displaystyle=E\{\hat{\tau}^{s}(z)-\tau^{s}(z)\}^{2}=\frac{1}{(T-p)^{2}}\left[\sum_{t=p+1}^{T}E\left\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\right\}^{2}+\sum_{t\neq t^{\prime}}E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}\right].

In the proof, we will respectively maximize
E\left\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\right\}^{2}
,
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}
,
E\left\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\right\}^{2}
and
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}
under Assumptions
1
–
4
.

(1) Recall that

\displaystyle N^{2}E\left\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(r_{q}^{-J_{t}}-1)\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})-\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\right\}^{2}+2r_{q}^{-J_{t}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\right\}

\displaystyle+r_{q}^{-J_{t}}(q^{-J_{t}}-1)\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\right\}^{2}+r_{q}^{-J_{t}}(\bar{q}^{-J_{t}}-1)\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\right\}^{2}.

The last two terms in
Equation

S2
are maximized when
|Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})|=|Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})|=B
for specific
t
and
i=1,\ldots,N
, where
B
represents the bound of potential outcomes as stated in
Assumption

4
. For simplicity, let us denote
a_{i}=Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})
and
b_{i}=Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})
. The remaining term can be expressed as:

\displaystyle(r_{q}^{-J_{t}}-1)\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}a_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{N}b_{i}\right)^{2}+2r_{q}^{-J_{t}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}a_{i}b_{i}.

(S2)

Without loss of generality, we can assume that
\sum_{i=1}^{N}a_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{N}b_{i}\geq 0
. If this is not the case, we can simply switch
\{a_{i}\}
and
\{b_{i}\}
to satisfy this condition with the value of
Equation

S2
unchanged.

Next, consider two new sets defined as
\{a_{i}^{\prime}\}
and
\{b_{i}^{\prime}\}
:

\displaystyle a_{i}^{\prime}=a_{i},\quad b_{i}^{\prime}=b_{i},\quad

\displaystyle\text{if}\quad a_{i}\geq b_{i},\quad\max(a_{i},b_{i})\geq 0;

\displaystyle a_{i}^{\prime}=-b_{i},\quad b_{i}^{\prime}=-a_{i},\quad

\displaystyle\text{if}\quad a_{i}\geq b_{i},\quad\max(a_{i},b_{i})<0;

\displaystyle a_{i}^{\prime}=b_{i},\quad b_{i}^{\prime}=a_{i},\quad

\displaystyle\text{if}\quad a_{i}<b_{i},\quad\max(a_{i},b_{i})\geq 0;

\displaystyle a_{i}^{\prime}=-a_{i},\quad b_{i}^{\prime}=-b_{i},\quad

\displaystyle\text{if}\quad a_{i}<b_{i},\quad\max(a_{i},b_{i})<0.

Then,
\{a_{i}^{\prime}\}
and
\{b_{i}^{\prime}\}
satisfies
a_{i}^{\prime}\geq b_{i}^{\prime}
and
a_{i}^{\prime}\geq 0
. Additionally,
a_{i}^{\prime}-b_{i}^{\prime}\geq a_{i}-b_{i}
implies
\sum_{i=1}^{N}a_{i}^{\prime}-\sum_{i=1}^{N}b_{i}^{\prime}\geq\sum_{i=1}^{N}a_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{N}b_{i}\geq 0
, and
a_{i}^{\prime}b_{i}^{\prime}=a_{i}b_{i}
implies
\sum_{i=1}^{N}a_{i}^{\prime}b_{i}^{\prime}=\sum_{i=1}^{N}a_{i}b_{i}
.
Then we obtain
\{a_{i}^{\prime}\}
and
\{b_{i}^{\prime}\}
with
a_{i}^{\prime}\geq b_{i}^{\prime}
that yield a value of
Equation

S2
at least as large as that of the original sets.

Next, additionally consider two new sets defined as
\{a_{i}^{\prime\prime}\}
and
\{b_{i}^{\prime\prime}\}
:
a_{i}^{\prime\prime}=B
and
b_{i}^{\prime\prime}=B-(a_{i}^{\prime}-b_{i}^{\prime})
. We have
(\sum_{i=1}^{N}a_{i}^{\prime\prime}-\sum_{i=1}^{N}b_{i}^{\prime\prime})-(\sum_{i=1}^{N}a_{i}^{\prime}-\sum_{i=1}^{N}b_{i}^{\prime})=0
given that
a_{i}^{\prime\prime}-b_{i}^{\prime\prime}=a_{i}^{\prime}-b_{i}^{\prime}
; and
\sum_{i=1}^{N}a_{i}^{\prime\prime}b_{i}^{\prime\prime}\geq\sum_{i=1}^{N}a_{i}^{\prime}b_{i}
given that
a_{i}^{\prime\prime}b_{i}^{\prime\prime}-a_{i}^{\prime}b_{i}^{\prime}=Bb_{i}^{\prime\prime}-Bb_{i}^{\prime}+Bb_{i}^{\prime}-a_{i}^{\prime}b_{i}^{\prime}=B^{2}-Ba_{i}^{\prime}+Bb_{i}^{\prime}-a_{i}^{\prime}b_{i}^{\prime}=(B-a_{i}^{\prime})(B+b_{i}^{\prime})\geq 0
. Then, we obtain two new sets
\{a_{i}^{\prime\prime}\}
and
\{b_{i}^{\prime\prime}\}
with
a_{i}^{\prime\prime}=B
and
b_{i}^{\prime\prime}\leq B
that yield a value of
Equation

S2
at least as large as that of the original sets.

Next, we maximize
Equation

S2
based on the sets
\{a_{i}^{\prime\prime}\}
and
\{b_{i}^{\prime\prime}\}
. For simplicity, we define
x=\sum_{i=1}^{N}b_{i}^{\prime\prime}
.
Equation

S2
can be expressed as

\displaystyle f(x)=(r_{q}^{-J_{t}}-1)(NB-x)^{2}+2r_{q}^{-J_{t}}Bx.

Expanding this yields

\displaystyle f(x)=(r_{q}^{-J_{t}}-1)x^{2}-2B(Nr_{q}^{-J_{t}}-N-2r_{q}^{-J_{t}})x+(r_{q}^{-J_{t}}-1)N^{2}B^{2},

where
x\in[-NB,NB]
. Given that
J_{t}\geq 1
and
r_{q}\in(0,1)
, we have
r_{q}^{-J_{t}}-1>0
.

We can calculate that
f(-NB)=\{4N^{2}(r_{q}^{-J_{t}}-1)-2Nr_{q}^{-J_{t}}\}B^{2}

and
f(NB)=2Nr_{q}^{-J_{t}}B^{2}
. When
f(-NB)\geq f(NB)
, i.e.,
N\geq(1-r_{q})^{-1}
, the maximum is achieved when
x=-NB
, which corresponds to
b_{i}=-B
for
i=1,\ldots,B
; when
f(-NB)\leq f(NB)
for
J_{t}=1,\ldots,p+1
, i.e.,
N\leq(1-r_{q}^{p+1})^{-1}
, the maximum is achieved when
x=NB
, which corresponds to
b_{i}=B
for
i=1,\ldots,N
.

In summary, when
N\geq(1-r_{q})^{-1}
, we have demonstrated that any original sets
\{a_{i}\}
and
\{b_{i}\}
yield a value of
Equation

S2
that is at most equal to that obtained when
Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=C
and
Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=-C
for specific
t
and
i=1,\ldots,N
, where
C=\pm B
. This is justified by the observation that the values of
Equation

S2
remain the same for
C=B
and
C=-B
.

Similarly, when
N\leq(1-r_{q}^{p+1})^{-1}
, we can show that any original sets
\{a_{i}\}
and
\{b_{i}\}
yield a value of
Equation

S2
that is at most equal to that achieved when
Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=C
for specific
t
and
i=1,\ldots,N
, where
C=\pm B
.

For the interaction term
\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)
, recall that

\displaystyle N^{2}E\left\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(r_{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})-\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\right\}\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})-\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\right\}

\displaystyle+r_{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\right\}+r_{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\right\}

\displaystyle+r_{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}(q^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})\right\}

\displaystyle+r_{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}(\bar{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})\right\}.

The last two terms are maximized when
Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})=Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})
and
|Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})|=|Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})|=B
for
t
,
t^{\prime}
,
z=0,1
and
i=1,\ldots,N
. For simplicity, let us denote
a_{i}=Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})
,
b_{i}=Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})
,
c_{i}=Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})
and
d_{i}=Y_{i,t^{\prime}}({q}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})
. The remaining term can be expressed as

\displaystyle(r_{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}a_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{N}b_{i}\right)\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}c_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{N}d_{i}\right)+r_{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}a_{i}d_{i}+r_{q}^{-J}\sum_{i=1}^{N}b_{i}c_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}(r_{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}a_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{N}c_{i}\right)^{2}-\frac{1}{2}(r_{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}b_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{N}d_{i}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}(r_{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}a_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{N}d_{i}\right)^{2}+r_{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}a_{i}d_{i}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}(r_{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}c_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{N}b_{i}\right)^{2}+r_{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}b_{i}c_{i}.

The terms in the second line are maximized when
\sum_{i=1}^{N}a_{i}=\sum_{i=1}^{N}c_{i}
and
\sum_{i=1}^{N}b_{i}=\sum_{i=1}^{N}d_{i}
; the terms in the third line can be interpreted as half of
Equation

S2
with
b_{i}
substituted by
d_{i}
and
J_{t}
substituted by
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}
; the terms in the last line can be similarly interpreted as half of
Equation

S2
with
a_{i}
substituted by
c_{i}
and
J_{t}
substituted by
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}
. Based on the results above, this function is maximized when
a_{i}=c_{i}=C
,
b_{i}=d_{i}=-C
with
C=\pm B
when
N\geq(1-r_{q})^{-1}
; it is maximized when
a_{i}=c_{i}=C
,
b_{i}=d_{i}=C
with
C=\pm B
when
N\leq(1-r_{q}^{p+1})^{-1}
.

In summary, when
N\geq(1-r_{q})^{-1}
,
\mathrm{risk}^{d}(q)
is maximized under
Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=C_{1},Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=-C_{1},Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=C_{2},Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=-C_{2}
with
C_{1}=\pm B
,
C_{2}=\pm B
and
B
is the bound of potential outcomes as stated in
Assumption

4
; when
N\leq(1-r_{q}^{p+1})^{-1}
,
\mathrm{risk}^{d}(q)
is minimized under
Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=C_{1},Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=C_{1},Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=C_{2},Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=C_{2}
with
C_{1}=\pm B
and
C_{2}=\pm B
.

Combining results of
q_{1}
and
q_{2}
, we can obtain that when
N\geq(1-\max\{r_{q_{1}},r_{q_{2}}\})^{-1}
,
\mathop{\arg\max}_{\mathbb{Y}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathcal{L}(1,0)=\{Y_{i,t}(\mathbf{q}_{(t-p):t},\mathbf{z}_{i,(t-p):t})\mid Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=C_{1},Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=-C_{1},Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=C_{2},Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=-C_{2}\}
with
C_{1}=\pm B
,
C_{2}=\pm B
; when
N\leq(1-\min\{r_{q_{1}}^{p+1},r_{q_{2}}^{p+1}\})^{-1}
,
\mathop{\arg\max}_{\mathbb{Y}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathcal{L}(1,0)=\{Y_{i,t}(\mathbf{q}_{(t-p):t},\mathbf{z}_{i,(t-p):t})\mid Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=C_{1},Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=C_{1},Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=C_{2},Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=C_{2}\}
with
C_{1}=\pm B
and
C_{2}=\pm B
.

(2) Recall that

\displaystyle N^{2}E\left\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(r_{q_{1}}^{-J_{t}}-1)\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}^{2}+(r_{q_{2}}^{-J_{t}}-1)\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{N}Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}

\displaystyle+r_{q_{1}}^{-J_{t}}(q_{1,z}^{-J_{t}}-1)\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}^{2}+r_{q_{2}}^{-J_{t}}(q_{2,z}^{-J_{t}}-1)\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\{Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})\right\}^{2}.

All terms in this function are maximized under
\{Y_{i,t}({q}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1}):Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})=Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{z}\mathbf{1})=C_{3}\}
with
C_{3}=\pm B
. The proof can be extended for
E\{\mathbf{S}_{i,t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{i,t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}
. Combining results of
z=0
and
z=1
, we obtain that
\mathop{\arg\max}_{\mathbb{Y}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathcal{L}(0,1)=\{Y_{i,t}(\mathbf{q}_{(t-p):t},\mathbf{z}_{i,(t-p):t})\mid Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=C_{3},Y_{i,t}({q_{1}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=C_{4},Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{1})=C_{3},Y_{i,t}({q_{2}}\mathbf{1},{}\mathbf{0})=C_{4}\}
with
C_{3}=\pm B
and
C_{4}=\pm B
.

(3) With
\mathcal{L}(\psi_{d},\psi_{s})=\psi_{d}\mathcal{L}(1,0)+\psi_{s}\mathcal{L}(0,1)
, where
\psi_{d}>0
and
\psi_{s}>0
, the maximum is obtained when
\mathbb{Y}
satisfies the conditions in both (1) and (2).
∎

Now, we can prove
Theorem

1
.

Proof of
Theorem

1
.

(1) When
N\geq(1-\max\{r_{q_{1}},r_{q_{2}}\})^{-1}
and
\mathbb{Y}^{*}\in\arg\max_{\mathbb{Y}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathcal{L}(1,0)
as shown in
Lemma

S2
, we have

\displaystyle E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q_{1})\}^{2}+E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q_{2})\}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4(r_{q_{1}}^{-J_{t}}+r_{q_{2}}^{-J_{t}}-2)B^{2}+\left\{r_{q_{1}}^{-J_{t}}(q_{1}^{-J_{t}}+\bar{q}_{1}^{-J_{t}}-4)+r_{q_{2}}^{-J_{t}}(q_{2}^{-J_{t}}+\bar{q}_{2}^{-J_{t}}-4)\right\}\frac{B^{2}}{N},

\displaystyle E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q_{1})\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q_{1})\}+E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q_{2})\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q_{2})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4(r_{q_{1}}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}+r_{q_{2}}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-2)B^{2}+\left\{r_{q_{1}}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}(q_{1}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}+\bar{q}_{1}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-4)+r_{q_{2}}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}(q_{2}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}+\bar{q}_{2}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-4)\right\}\frac{B^{2}}{N},

while when
N\leq(1-\min\{r_{q_{1}}^{p+1},r_{q_{2}}^{p+1}\})^{-1}
, we have

\displaystyle E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q_{1})\}^{2}+E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q_{2})\}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\{r_{q_{1}}^{-J_{t}}(q_{1}^{-J_{t}}+\bar{q}_{1}^{-J_{t}})+r_{q_{2}}^{-J_{t}}(q_{2}^{-J_{t}}+\bar{q}_{2}^{-J_{t}})\right\}\frac{B^{2}}{N},

\displaystyle E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q_{1})\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q_{1})\}+E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q_{2})\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q_{2})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\{r_{q_{1}}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}(q_{1}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}+\bar{q}_{1}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}})+r_{q_{2}}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}(q_{2}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}+\bar{q}_{2}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}})\right\}\frac{B^{2}}{N}.

Letting
J=J_{t}
or
J=J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}
,
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q_{1})\}^{2}+E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q_{2})\}^{2}
or
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q_{1})\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q_{1})\}+E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q_{2})\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q_{2})\}
becomes

\displaystyle 4(r_{q_{1}}^{-J}+r_{q_{2}}^{-J}-2)B^{2}+\left\{r_{q_{1}}^{-J}(q_{1}^{-J}+\bar{q}_{1}^{-J}-4)+r_{q_{2}}^{-J}(q_{2}^{-J}+\bar{q}_{2}^{-J}-4)\right\}\frac{B^{2}}{N}

(S3)

when
N\geq(1-\max\{r_{q_{1}},r_{q_{2}}\})^{-1}
and

\displaystyle\left\{r_{q_{1}}^{-J}(q_{1}^{-J}+\bar{q}_{1}^{-J})+r_{q_{2}}^{-J}(q_{2}^{-J}+\bar{q}_{2}^{-J})\right\}\frac{B^{2}}{N}

(S4)

when
N\leq(1-\min\{r_{q_{1}}^{p+1},r_{q_{2}}^{p+1}\})^{-1}
.
Given design
\mathbb{T}
, define
\mathcal{J}_{j}
as the sum of the number of time points
t
such that
J_{t}=j
and the number of pairs
(t,t^{\prime})
with
t\neq t^{\prime}
such that
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=j
. Combining terms from
j=1
to
p+1
, and further letting
\zeta_{j}=r_{q_{1}}^{-j}q_{1}^{-j}+r_{q_{1}}^{-j}\bar{q}_{1}^{-j}+r_{q_{2}}^{-j}q_{2}^{-j}+r_{q_{2}}^{-j}\bar{q}_{2}^{-j}
, we know

\displaystyle\mathop{\max}_{\mathbb{Y}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathcal{L}(1,0)=\sum_{j=1}^{p+1}\mathcal{J}_{j}\left\{4(r_{q_{1}}^{-j}+r_{q_{2}}^{-j})B^{2}(1-N^{-1})-8B^{2}+\zeta_{j}B^{2}/N\right\}

when
N\geq(1-\max\{r_{q_{1}},r_{q_{2}}\})^{-1}
, and

\displaystyle\mathop{\max}_{\mathbb{Y}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathcal{L}(1,0)=\sum_{j=1}^{p+1}\mathcal{J}_{j}\left\{\zeta_{j}B^{2}/N\right\}

when
N\leq(1-\min\{r_{q_{1}}^{p+1},r_{q_{2}}^{p+1}\})^{-1}
.

(2) When
\mathbb{Y}^{*}\in\arg\max_{\mathbb{Y}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathcal{L}(0,1)
as shown in
Lemma

S2
, we have

\displaystyle E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},1)\}^{2}+E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},0)\}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2(r_{q_{1}}^{-J_{t}}+r_{q_{2}}^{-J_{t}})B^{2}(1-N^{-1})+(r_{q_{1}}^{-J_{t}}q_{1}^{-J_{t}}+r_{q_{1}}^{-J_{t}}\bar{q}_{1}^{-J_{t}}+r_{q_{2}}^{-J_{t}}q_{2}^{-J_{t}}+r_{q_{2}}^{-J_{t}}\bar{q}_{2}^{-J_{t}})B^{2}/N,

\displaystyle E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},1)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},1)\}+E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},0)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},0)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2(r_{q_{1}}^{-J^{\circ}_{t,t^{\prime}}}+r_{q_{2}}^{-J^{\circ}_{t,t^{\prime}}})B^{2}(1-N^{-1})

\displaystyle+(r_{q_{1}}^{-J^{\circ}_{t,t^{\prime}}}q_{1}^{-J^{\circ}_{t,t^{\prime}}}+r_{q_{1}}^{-J^{\circ}_{t,t^{\prime}}}\bar{q}_{1}^{-J^{\circ}_{t,t^{\prime}}}+r_{q_{2}}^{-J^{\circ}_{t,t^{\prime}}}q_{2}^{-J^{\circ}_{t,t^{\prime}}}+r_{q_{2}}^{-J^{\circ}_{t,t^{\prime}}}\bar{q}_{2}^{-J^{\circ}_{t,t^{\prime}}})B^{2}/N.

Similarly letting
J=J_{t}
or
J=J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}
,
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},1)\}^{2}+E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},0)\}^{2}
or
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},1)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},1)\}+E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},0)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},0)\}
becomes

\displaystyle 2(r_{q_{1}}^{-J}+r_{q_{2}}^{-J})B^{2}(1-N^{-1})+(r_{q_{1}}^{-J}q_{1}^{-J}+r_{q_{1}}^{-J}\bar{q}_{1}^{-J}+r_{q_{2}}^{-J}q_{2}^{-J}+r_{q_{2}}^{-J}\bar{q}_{2}^{-J})B^{2}/N.

(S5)

Further considering
\zeta_{j}=r_{q_{1}}^{-j}q_{1}^{-j}+r_{q_{1}}^{-j}\bar{q}_{1}^{-j}+r_{q_{2}}^{-j}q_{2}^{-j}+r_{q_{2}}^{-j}\bar{q}_{2}^{-j}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathop{\max}_{\mathbb{Y}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathcal{L}(0,1)=\sum_{j=1}^{p+1}\mathcal{J}_{j}\left\{2(r_{q_{1}}^{-j}+r_{q_{2}}^{-j})B^{2}(1-N^{-1})+\zeta_{j}B^{2}/N\right\}.

(3) Based on
\mathcal{L}(\psi_{d},\psi_{s})=\psi_{d}\mathcal{L}(1,0)+\psi_{s}\mathcal{L}(0,1)
, and
\mathcal{L}(1,0)
and
\mathcal{L}(0,1)
can reach their maximum simultaneously, we have

\displaystyle\mathop{\max}_{\mathbb{Y}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathcal{L}(\psi_{d},\psi_{s})

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{p+1}\mathcal{J}_{j}\left\{(4\psi_{d}+2\psi_{s})(r_{q_{1}}^{-j}+r_{q_{2}}^{-j})B^{2}(1-N^{-1})-8\psi_{d}B^{2}+\zeta_{j}B^{2}/N\right\}

\displaystyle=B^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{p+1}\mathcal{J}_{j}\left\{(4\psi_{d}+2\psi_{s})(r_{q_{1}}^{-j}+r_{q_{2}}^{-j})(1-N^{-1})-8\psi_{d}+\zeta_{j}N^{-1}\right\}

when
N\geq(1-\max\{r_{q_{1}},r_{q_{2}}\})^{-1}
, and

\displaystyle\mathop{\max}_{\mathbb{Y}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathcal{L}(\psi_{d},\psi_{s})

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{p+1}\mathcal{J}_{j}\left\{2\psi_{s}(r_{q_{1}}^{-j}+r_{q_{2}}^{-j})B^{2}(1-N^{-1})+\zeta_{j}B^{2}/N\right\}

\displaystyle=B^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{p+1}\mathcal{J}_{j}\left\{2\psi_{s}(r_{q_{1}}^{-j}+r_{q_{2}}^{-j})(1-N^{-1})+\zeta_{j}N^{-1}\right\}

when
N\leq(1-\min\{r_{q_{1}}^{p+1},r_{q_{2}}^{p+1}\})^{-1}
.

(i) As
N\to\infty
, the term with
N^{-1}
becomes negligible, allowing us to focus on the remaining terms. For each
j
, these remaining terms are proportional to
r_{q_{1}}^{-j}+r_{q_{2}}^{-j}
. This expression reaches its minimum when
r_{q_{1}}=r_{q_{2}}=0.5
.

(ii) When
q_{1}+q_{2}=1
, although the term with
N^{-1}
is non-negligible, the term
\zeta_{j}
is proportional to
r_{q_{1}}^{-j}+r_{q_{2}}^{-j}
. Similar to the previous case, this expression is minimized at
r_{q_{1}}=r_{q_{2}}=0.5
.
∎

D
Proof of Theorem
2

When potential outcomes
\mathbb{Y}^{*}\in\arg\max_{\mathbb{Y}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathcal{L}(\psi_{d},\psi_{s})
shown in
Lemma

S2
and
r_{q_{1}}=r_{q_{2}}=0.5
, the expectations in
Lemma

S1
can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\}^{2}&=4(2^{J_{t}}-1)B^{2}+2^{J_{t}}(q^{-J_{t}}+\bar{q}^{-J_{t}}-4)\frac{B^{2}}{N},\quad\text{when}\quad N\geq 2;\\
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}&=4(2^{J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-1)B^{2}+2^{J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}(q^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}+\bar{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}-4)\frac{B^{2}}{N},\quad\text{when}\quad N\geq 2;\\
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\}^{2}&=2^{J_{t}}(q^{-J_{t}}+\bar{q}^{-J_{t}})\frac{B^{2}}{N},\quad\text{when}\quad N=1;\\
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}&=2^{J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}(q^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}}+\bar{q}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}})\frac{B^{2}}{N},\quad\text{when}\quad N=1;\\
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\}^{2}&=2^{J_{t}+1}B^{2}+2^{J_{t}}(q_{1,z}^{-J_{t}}+q_{2,z}^{-J_{t}}-2)\frac{B^{2}}{N},\\
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}&=2^{J_{t,t^{\prime}}+1}B^{2}+2^{J_{t,t^{\prime}}}(q_{1,z}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}}+q_{2,z}^{-J_{t,t^{\prime}}}-2)\frac{B^{2}}{N}.\end{aligned}

(S6)

All quantities in
Equation

S6
increase as
J_{t}
or
J_{t,t^{\prime}}
increases, given that
q_{1},q_{2}\in(0,1)
. In the remaining part of the proof, we define
\alpha_{J}(q)
and
\beta_{J}(z)
to simplify the notation:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \alpha_{J}(q)&=4(2^{J}-1)B^{2}+2^{J}(q^{-J}+\bar{q}^{-J}-4)\frac{B^{2}}{N},\quad\text{when}\quad N\geq 2;\\
\alpha_{J}(q)&=2^{J}(q^{-J}+\bar{q}^{-J})\frac{B^{2}}{N},\quad\text{when}\quad N=1;\\
\beta_{J}(z)&=2^{J+1}B^{2}+2^{J}(q_{1,z}^{-J}+q_{2,z}^{-J}-2)\frac{B^{2}}{N}.\end{aligned}

(S7)

Notably,
\alpha_{J}(q)
equals to
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)^{2}\}
when
J_{t}=J
and
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}
when
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=J
. Similarly,
\beta_{J}(z)
equals to
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)^{2}\}
when
J_{t}=J
and
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}
when
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=J
.

Additionally, since the objective function
\mathcal{L}(\psi_{d},\psi_{s})
is simply a weighted sum of
\mathcal{L}(1,0)
and
\mathcal{L}(0,1)
, we first analyze
\mathcal{L}(1,0)
and
\mathcal{L}(0,1)
. When the results from these two components are identical, the results can be directly extended to
\mathcal{L}(\psi_{d},\psi_{s})
.

Lemma S3
.

Under Assumptions
1
–
4
and
r_{q_{1}}=r_{q_{2}}=0.5
, any design
\mathbb{T}
that minimizes
\max_{\mathbb{Y}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathcal{L}(\psi_{d},\psi_{s})
must satisfy
t_{1}\geq p+2
and
t_{L}\leq T-p
.

Proof of
Lemma

S3
.

Suppose there exists a minimax optimal design
\mathbb{T}=\{t_{0}=1,t_{1},t_{2},\ldots,t_{L}\}
such that
t_{1}\leq p+1
. Then we define
\tilde{\mathbb{T}}=\{t_{0},t_{2},t_{3},\ldots,t_{L}\}
and we want to prove that under
\tilde{\mathbb{T}}
, the objective function will decrease compared to
\mathbb{T}
, which suggests that
\mathbb{T}
is not the minimax optimal design.

(1) We first consider
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\}^{2}-E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}^{2}
and
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\}^{2}-E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}^{2}
. We divide
\{p+1,\ldots,T\}
into
[p+1,t_{1}+p-1]
and
[t_{1}+p,T]
.

(1.1) For
t\in[p+1,t_{1}+p-1]
. Under this circumstance,
t_{1}\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}^{p}(t)
but
t_{1}\notin\mathcal{F}_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}^{p}(t)
. It means
\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}^{p}(t)-\{t_{1}\}=\mathcal{F}_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}^{p}(t)
and
\tilde{J}_{t}=J_{t}-1
, where
\tilde{J}_{t}=|\mathcal{F}_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}^{p}(t)|
. Based on
Equation

S6
and
\tilde{J}_{t}<J_{t}
, we have
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\}^{2}-E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}^{2}>0
and
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\}^{2}-E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}^{2}>0
.

(1.2) For
t\geq t_{1}+p
,
|\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}^{p}(t)|=|\mathcal{F}_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}^{p}(t)|
, because either (i)
\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}(t-p)=t_{1}
and in this case
\mathcal{F}_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}(t-p)=t_{0}
, which means
\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}^{p}(t)-\{t_{1}\}=\mathcal{F}_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}^{p}(t)-\{t_{0}\}
; or (ii)
\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}(t-p)\geq t_{2}
, in which case
\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}^{p}(t)=\mathcal{F}_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}^{p}(t)
. Under both cases, we have
J_{t}=\tilde{J}_{t}
, which implies that
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\}^{2}-E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}^{2}=0
and
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\}^{2}-E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}^{2}=0
.

Based on (1.1) and (1.2), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=p+1}^{T}E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\}^{2}-\sum_{t=p+1}^{T}E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}^{2}

\displaystyle>0,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=p+1}^{T}E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\}^{2}-\sum_{t=p+1}^{T}E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}^{2}

\displaystyle>0.

(2) Consider
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}-E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}
and
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}-E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}
.

(2.1) For any
t
and
t^{\prime}
such that
p+1\leq t<t^{\prime}\leq t_{1}+p-1
,
t_{1}\in O_{\mathbb{T}}(t,t^{\prime})
but
t_{1}\notin O_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}(t,t^{\prime})
, where
O_{\mathbb{T}}(t,t^{\prime})=\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}^{p}(t)\cap\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}^{p}(t^{\prime})
. Then,
\tilde{J}_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}-1
. As a result,
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t}^{\prime}(\mathbb{T},q)\}-E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\mathbf{D}_{t}^{\prime}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}>0
and
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t}^{\prime}(\mathbb{T},z)\}-E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\mathbf{S}_{t}^{\prime}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}>0
.

(2.2) For any
p+1\leq t<t^{\prime}\leq T
and
t^{\prime}\geq t_{1}+p
, either (i)
\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}(t^{\prime}-p)=t_{1}
and in this case
\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}(t^{\prime}-p)=t_{0}
, which implies that
O_{\mathbb{T}}(t,t^{\prime})-\{t_{1}\}=O_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}(t,t^{\prime})-\{t_{0}\}
; or (ii)
\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}(t^{\prime}-p)=t_{2}
, in which case
O_{\mathbb{T}}(t,t^{\prime})=O_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}(t,t^{\prime})
. In both cases,
|O_{\mathbb{T}}(t,t^{\prime})|=|O_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}(t,t^{\prime})|
, which implies that
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=\tilde{J}_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}
. Then,
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t}^{\prime}(\mathbb{T},q)\}-E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\mathbf{D}_{t}^{\prime}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}=0
and
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t}^{\prime}(\mathbb{T},z)\}-E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\mathbf{S}_{t}^{\prime}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}=0
.

Based on (2.1) and (2.2), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{p+1\leq t<t^{\prime}\leq T}E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t}^{\prime}(\mathbb{T},q)\}-\sum_{p+1\leq t<t^{\prime}\leq T}E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\mathbf{D}_{t}^{\prime}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}

\displaystyle>0,

\displaystyle\sum_{p+1\leq t<t^{\prime}\leq T}E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t}^{\prime}(\mathbb{T},z)\}-\sum_{p+1\leq t<t^{\prime}\leq T}E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\mathbf{S}_{t}^{\prime}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}

\displaystyle>0.

As a result, either
\mathcal{L}(0,1)
or
\mathcal{L}(1,0)
will decrease under
\tilde{\mathbb{T}}
compared to
\mathbb{T}
. This result can extended to
\mathcal{L}(\psi_{d},\psi_{s})
for it is the weighted sum of
\mathcal{L}(0,1)
and
\mathcal{L}(1,0)
.
Similarly, we can prove that
t_{L}\leq T-p
.
∎

Lemma S4
.

Under Assumptions
1
–
4
and
r_{q_{1}}=r_{q_{2}}=0.5
, any design
\mathbb{T}
that minimizes
\max_{\mathbb{Y}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathcal{L}(\psi_{d},\psi_{s})
must satisfy
t_{l+1}-t_{l-1}\geq p
, for
l=1,\ldots,L
.

Proof of
Lemma

S4
.

By Lemma
S3
, when
l=1
,
t_{2}-t_{0}>t_{1}-t_{0}\geq p+1
; when
l=L
,
t_{L+1}-t_{L-1}>t_{L+1}-t_{L}\geq p+1
. For
2\leq l\leq L-1
, we will prove the result by contradiction.

Suppose there exists a minimax optimal design
\mathbb{T}
, and
\exists 2\leq l\leq L-1
, s.t.
t_{l+1}-t_{l-1}\leq p-1
. Denote
\mathbb{L}=\{l\in\{2:L-1\}\mid t_{l+1}-t_{l-1}\leq p-1\}
and let
l^{\prime}=\max_{l\in\mathbb{L}}l
. We know that
l^{\prime}\leq L-1
,
t_{l^{\prime}+1}-t_{l^{\prime}-1}\leq p-1
and
t_{l^{\prime}+2}-t_{l^{\prime}}\geq p
. We then construct another design
\tilde{\mathbb{T}}=\{\tilde{t}_{0}=1,\tilde{t}_{1}=t_{1},\ldots,\tilde{t}_{l^{\prime}-1}=t_{l^{\prime}-1},\tilde{t}_{l^{\prime}}=t_{l^{\prime}+1},\ldots,\tilde{t}_{L-1}=t_{L}\}
.

(1) We first consider
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\}^{2}-E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}^{2}
and
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\}^{2}-E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}^{2}
.

(1.1) When
t\leq t_{l^{\prime}}-1
,
\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}^{p}(t)=\mathcal{F}_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}^{p}(t)
and
J_{t}=\tilde{J}_{t}
, which further implies that
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\}^{2}-E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}^{2}=0
and
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\}^{2}-E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}^{2}=0
.

(1.2) When
t\leq t_{l^{\prime}}+p-1
,
t_{l^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}^{p}(t)
but
t_{l^{\prime}}\notin\mathcal{F}_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}^{p}(t)
. We then have
\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}^{p}(t)-\{t_{l^{\prime}}\}=\mathcal{F}_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}^{p}(t)
(
J_{t}=\tilde{J}_{t}+1
) and
|\mathcal{F}_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}^{p}(t)|\geq 1
(
\tilde{J}_{t}\geq 1
). By
Equation

S6
,
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\}^{2}-E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}^{2}>0
and
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\}^{2}-E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}^{2}>0
.

(1.3) When
t_{l^{\prime}}+p\leq t\leq T
, either
\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}(t-p)=t_{l^{\prime}}
or
\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}(t-p)\geq t_{l^{\prime}+1}
. In both cases,
|\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}^{p}(t)|=|\mathcal{F}_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}^{p}(t)|
(
J_{t}=\tilde{J}_{t}
), which implies that
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\}^{2}-E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}^{2}=0
and
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\}^{2}-E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}^{2}=0
. Combing the above arguments, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=p+1}^{T}E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\}^{2}-\sum_{t=p+1}^{T}E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}^{2}

\displaystyle>0,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=p+1}^{T}E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\}^{2}-\sum_{t=p+1}^{T}E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}^{2}

\displaystyle>0.

(2) We then consider the cross-product terms, which are associated with
O_{\mathbb{T}}(t,t^{\prime})
and
O_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}(t,t^{\prime})
. The time period
[p+1,T]
can be divided by
t_{l^{\prime}-1}
,
t_{l^{\prime}}
and
t_{l^{\prime}}+p
into four sub time periods:
[p+1,t_{l^{\prime}-1}-1]
,
[t_{l^{\prime}-1},t_{l^{\prime}}-1]
,
[t_{l^{\prime}},t_{l^{\prime}}+p-1]
and
[t_{l^{\prime}}+p,T]
. Without loss of generality, we assume
t<t^{\prime}
. Define
\tilde{J}_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=|O_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}(t,t^{\prime})|
.

(2.1) When
t\in[p+1,t_{l^{\prime}-1}-1]
, for any
t^{\prime}>t
, the overlapping points are unchanged, and we have
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=\tilde{J}_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}
. It implies that
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}-E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}=0
and
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}-E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}=0
.

(2.2) When
t\in[t_{l^{\prime}-1},t_{l^{\prime}}-1]
and
t^{\prime}\in[t+1,t_{l^{\prime}}+p-1]
,
t_{l^{\prime}-1}
is in both
O_{\mathbb{T}}(t,t^{\prime})
and
O_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}(t,t^{\prime})
but
t_{l^{\prime}}
is in neither, which suggests that
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=\tilde{J}_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}
and then
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}-E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}=0
,
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}-E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}=0
.

(2.3) When
t\in[t_{l^{\prime}-1},t_{l^{\prime}}-1]
and
t^{\prime}\in[t_{l^{\prime}}+p,t_{l^{\prime}+1}+p-1]
,
t_{l^{\prime}-1}
is in
O_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}(t,t^{\prime})
but not in
O_{\mathbb{T}}(t,t^{\prime})
, which means that
O_{\mathbb{T}}(t,t^{\prime})=O_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}(t,t^{\prime})-\{t_{l^{\prime}-1}\}
(
J_{t,t^{\prime}}=\tilde{J}_{t,t^{\prime}}-1
). Also,
\forall a\in\mathcal{F}_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}^{p}(t^{\prime})
, we have
a\geq t_{l^{\prime}-1}
, given that
t^{\prime}\geq t_{l^{\prime}}+p>t_{l^{\prime}-1}+p
;
\forall a\in\mathcal{F}_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}^{p}(t)
, we have
a\leq t_{l^{\prime}-1}
, given that
t\leq t_{l^{\prime}}-1
; and
t_{l^{\prime}-1}\in O_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}(t,t^{\prime})
, given that
t_{l^{\prime}-1}\in\mathcal{F}_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}^{p}(t)
when
t=t_{l^{\prime}-1}
and
t_{l^{\prime}-1}\in\mathcal{F}_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}^{p}(t^{\prime})
when
t^{\prime}=t_{l^{\prime}}+p
. Thus,
O_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}(t,t^{\prime})=\{t_{l^{\prime}-1}\}
and
O_{\mathbb{T}}(t,t^{\prime})=\emptyset
. Therefore,

\displaystyle E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}-E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}

\displaystyle=-\alpha_{1}(q),

\displaystyle E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}-E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}

\displaystyle=-\beta_{1}(z).

(2.4) When
t\in[t_{l^{\prime}-1},t_{l^{\prime}}-1]
and
t^{\prime}\in[t_{l^{\prime}+1}+p,T]
, both
O_{\mathbb{T}}(t,t^{\prime})
and
O_{\mathbb{\mathbb{T}}}(t,t^{\prime})
are empty sets, and then
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}-E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}=0
and
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}-E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}=0
.

(2.5) When
t\in[t_{l^{\prime}},t_{l^{\prime}}+p-1]
and
t^{\prime}\in[t,t_{l^{\prime}}+p-1]
,
t_{l^{\prime}}\in O_{\mathbb{T}}(t,t^{\prime})
but
t_{l^{\prime}}\notin O_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}(t,t^{\prime})
. The other overlapping points are unchanged. Furthermore,
t_{l^{\prime}-1}\in O_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}(t,t^{\prime})
. As a result,
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}-1=\tilde{J}_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}\geq 1
. Further notice that
\alpha_{J+1}(q)-\alpha_{J}(q)
and
\beta_{J+1}(z)-\beta_{J}(z)
increase with
J
when
J\geq 1
. Thus,

\displaystyle E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}-E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}

\displaystyle\geq\alpha_{2}(q)-\alpha_{1}(q),

\displaystyle E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}-E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}

\displaystyle\geq\beta_{2}(z)-\beta_{1}(z).

(2.6) When
t\in[t_{l^{\prime}},t_{l^{\prime}}+p-1]
and
t^{\prime}\in[t_{l^{\prime}}+p,T]
, either
\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}^{p}(t^{\prime}-p)=t_{l^{\prime}}
or
\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}^{p}(t^{\prime}-p)\geq t_{l^{\prime}+1}
. Under both cases,
|O_{\mathbb{T}}(t,t^{\prime})|=|O_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}(t,t^{\prime})|
and as a result,
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}-E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}=0
and
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}-E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}=0
.

(2.7) When
t_{l^{\prime}}+p\leq t<t^{\prime}\leq T
, either
\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}^{p}(t^{\prime}-p)=t_{l^{\prime}}
or
\mathcal{F}_{\mathbb{T}}^{p}(t^{\prime}-p)\geq t_{l^{\prime}+1}
. Under both cases,
|O_{\mathbb{T}}(t,t^{\prime})|=|O_{\tilde{\mathbb{T}}}(t,t^{\prime})|
and as a result,
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}-E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}=0
and
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}-E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}=0
.

The main difference occurs in (2.3) and (2.5). Combing (2.1)–(2.7), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{p+1\leq t<t^{\prime}\leq T}E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}-\sum_{p+1\leq t<t^{\prime}\leq T}E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{l^{\prime}-1}\leq t\leq t_{l}^{\prime}-1,t_{l^{\prime}}+p\leq t^{\prime}\leq t_{l^{\prime}+1}+p-1}\left[E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}-E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{t_{l^{\prime}}\leq t<t^{\prime}\leq t_{l^{\prime}}+p-1}\left[E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}-E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)\}\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-(t_{l^{\prime}}-t_{l^{\prime}-1})(t_{l^{\prime}+1}-t_{l^{\prime}})\alpha_{1}(q)+\frac{p(p-1)}{2}\{\alpha_{2}(q)-\alpha_{1}(q)\}

\displaystyle>

\displaystyle\frac{p(p-1)}{4}\{2\alpha_{2}(q)-3\alpha_{1}(q)\}>0,

and

\displaystyle\sum_{p+1\leq t<t^{\prime}\leq T}E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}-\sum_{p+1\leq t<t^{\prime}\leq T}E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{l^{\prime}-1}\leq t\leq t_{l}^{\prime}-1,t_{l^{\prime}}+p\leq t^{\prime}\leq t_{l^{\prime}+1}+p-1}\left[E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}-E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{t_{l^{\prime}}\leq t<t^{\prime}\leq t_{l^{\prime}}+p-1}\left[E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}-E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)\}\right]

\displaystyle\geq-(t_{l^{\prime}}-t_{l^{\prime}-1})(t_{l^{\prime}+1}-t_{l^{\prime}})\beta_{1}(z)+\frac{p(p-1)}{2}\{\beta_{2}(z)-\beta_{1}(z)\}>\frac{p(p-1)}{4}\{2\beta_{2}(z)-3\beta_{1}(z)\}

\displaystyle>0.

The inequality holds as
(t_{l^{\prime}}-t_{l^{\prime}-1})(t_{l^{\prime}+1}-t_{l^{\prime}})<p(p-1)/4
when
t_{l^{\prime}+1}-t_{l^{\prime}-1}\leq p-1
. Therefore,
\mathrm{risk}^{d}(\mathbb{T},q)>\mathrm{risk}^{d}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},q)
and
\mathrm{risk}^{s}(\mathbb{T},q)>\mathrm{risk}^{s}(\tilde{\mathbb{T}},z)
, which is a contradiction.

As a result, either
\mathcal{L}(0,1)
or
\mathcal{L}(1,0)
will decrease under
\tilde{\mathbb{T}}
compared to
\mathbb{T}
. This result can extended to
\mathcal{L}(\psi_{d},\psi_{s})
for it is the weighted sum of
\mathcal{L}(0,1)
and
\mathcal{L}(1,0)
.
∎

Based on
Lemma

S3
–
S4
, any minimax optimal design must satisfy
t_{1}\geq p+2
,
t_{L}\leq T-p
and
t_{l+1}-t_{l-1}\geq p
, for
l=1,\ldots,L
. Define
\mathcal{T}=\{\mathbb{T}\mid t_{1}\geq p+2,t_{L}\leq T-p,\text{ and }t_{l+1}-t_{l-1}\geq p\text{ for }l=1,\ldots,L\}
. Then solving the minimax problem
\min_{\mathbb{T}}\max_{\mathbb{Y}\in\mathcal{L}}\mathcal{L}(\psi_{d},\psi_{s})
is equivalent to solving
\min_{\mathbb{T}\in\mathcal{T}}\max_{\mathbb{Y}\in\mathcal{L}}\mathcal{L}(\psi_{d},\psi_{s})
.

Lemma S5
.

Under Assumptions
1
–
4
and
r_{q_{1}}=r_{q_{2}}=0.5
, when
\mathbb{T}\in\mathcal{T}
and
\mathbb{Y}\in\arg\max_{\mathbb{Y}\in\mathcal{Y}}\mathcal{L}(\psi_{d},\psi_{s})
,
\mathrm{risk}^{d}(q)
satisfies

\displaystyle(T-p)^{2}\mathrm{risk}^{d}(q)=\left\{\sum_{l=0}^{L}(t_{l+1}-t_{l})^{2}+(L-1)p^{2}+2p(t_{L}-t_{1})\right\}\alpha_{1}(q)

\displaystyle+Lp^{2}\{\alpha_{2}(q)-3\alpha_{1}(q)\}+\left(\sum_{l=2}^{L}\left[\{(p-t_{l}+t_{l-1})^{+}\}^{2}\right]\right)\{\alpha_{3}(q)-2\alpha_{2}(q)+\alpha_{1}(q)\}

where
\alpha_{J}(q)
(
J=1,2,3
) are defined in
Equation

S7
and

\mathrm{risk}^{s}(z)
satisfies

\displaystyle(T-p)^{2}\mathrm{risk}^{s}(z)=\left\{\sum_{l=0}^{L}(t_{l+1}-t_{l})^{2}+(L-1)p^{2}+2p(t_{L}-t_{1})\right\}\beta_{1}(z)

\displaystyle+Lp^{2}\{\beta_{2}(z)-2\beta_{1}(z)\}+\left(\sum_{l=2}^{L}\left[\{(p-t_{l}+t_{l-1})^{+}\}^{2}\right]\right)\{\beta_{3}(z)-2\beta_{2}(z)+\beta_{1}(z)\},

where
\beta_{J}(z)
(
J=1,2,3
) are defined in
Equation

S7
.

Proof of
Lemma

S5
.

When
\mathbb{T}\in\mathcal{T}
,
\mathrm{risk}^{d}(q)
satisfies

\displaystyle(T-p)^{2}\mathrm{risk}^{d}(q)=(T-p)^{2}E\{\hat{\tau}^{d}(q)-\tau^{d}(q)\}^{2}=E\left\{\sum_{t=p+1}^{T}\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\sum_{t^{\prime}=p+1}^{T}\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}p+1\leq t,t^{\prime}\leq T\\
\min\left\{t,t^{\prime}\right\}\leq t_{1}-1\end{subarray}}E\left\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\right\}+\sum_{l=1}^{L-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t_{l}\leq t,t^{\prime}\leq T\\
\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1\end{subarray}}E\left\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{t_{L}\leq t,t^{\prime}\leq T}E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\},

and
\mathrm{risk}^{s}(z)
satisifies

\displaystyle(T-p)^{2}\mathrm{risk}^{s}(z)=(T-p)^{2}E\{\hat{\tau}^{s}(z)-\tau^{s}(z)\}^{2}=E\left\{\sum_{t=p+1}^{T}\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\sum_{t^{\prime}=p+1}^{T}\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}p+1\leq t,t^{\prime}\leq T\\
\min\left\{t,t^{\prime}\right\}\leq t_{1}-1\end{subarray}}E\left\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\right\}+\sum_{l=1}^{L-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t_{l}\leq t,t^{\prime}\leq T\\
\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1\end{subarray}}E\left\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{t_{L}\leq t,t^{\prime}\leq T}E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}.

We consider the risk function under three cases. Notably, by
Lemma

S3
and
Lemma

S4
, any minimax optimal design
\mathbb{T}\in\mathcal{T}
satisfies
t_{1}\geq p+2
,
t_{L}\leq T-p
and
t_{l+1}-t_{l-1}\geq p
, for
l=1,\ldots,L
.

(1) For any
t,t^{\prime}
such that
p+1\leq\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{1}-1
,
p+1\leq\max(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{1}+p-1
, we have
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=1
; for any
t,t^{\prime}
such that
p+1\leq\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{1}-1
,
t_{1}+p\leq\max(t,t^{\prime})\leq T
, we have
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=0
,
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}=0
, and
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}=0
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}p+1\leq t,t^{\prime}\leq T\\
\min\left\{t,t^{\prime}\right\}\leq t_{1}-1\end{subarray}}E\left\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\right\}=\{(t_{1}-1)^{2}-p^{2}\}\alpha_{1}(q),

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}p+1\leq t,t^{\prime}\leq T\\
\min\left\{t,t^{\prime}\right\}\leq t_{1}-1\end{subarray}}E\left\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\right\}=\{(t_{1}-1)^{2}-p^{2}\}\beta_{1}(z).

(2) For any
l\in[L-1]
, we consider
t_{l}-t_{l-1}
and
t_{l+1}-t_{l}
. We need to consider the values of
E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}
and
E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}
when
t_{l}\leq\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1
and
t_{l}\leq\max(t,t^{\prime})\leq T
.

(2.1) When
t_{l}-t_{l-1}\geq p
and
t_{l+1}-t_{l}\geq p
, for
t_{l}\leq\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l}+p-1
and
t_{l}\leq\max(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l}+p-1
, we have
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=2
; for
t_{l}\leq\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1
and
t_{l}+p\leq\max(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}+p-1
, we have
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=1
; for
t_{l}\leq\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1
and
t_{l+1}+p\leq\max(t,t^{\prime})\leq T
, we have
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=0
. In these cases, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t_{l}\leq t,t^{\prime}\leq T\\
\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1\end{subarray}}E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}=\{(p+t_{l+1}-t_{l})^{2}-2p^{2}\}\alpha_{1}(q)+p^{2}\alpha_{2}(q),

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t_{l}\leq t,t^{\prime}\leq T\\
\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1\end{subarray}}E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}=\{(p+t_{l+1}-t_{l})^{2}-2p^{2}\}\beta_{1}(z)+p^{2}\beta_{2}(z).

(2.2) When
t_{l}-t_{l-1}\geq p
and
t_{l+1}-t_{l}<p
, for
t_{l}\leq\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1
and
t_{l}\leq\max(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l}+p+1
, we have
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=2
; for
t_{l}\leq\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1
and
t_{l}+p\leq\max(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}+p+1
, we have
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=1
; for
t_{l}\leq\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1
and
t_{l+1}-p\leq\max(t,t^{\prime})\leq T
, we have
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=0
. In these cases, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t_{l}\leq t,t^{\prime}\leq T\\
\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1\end{subarray}}E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\{(p+t_{l+1}-t_{l})^{2}-2p^{2}+(p-t_{l+1}+t_{l})^{2}\}\alpha_{1}(q)+\{p^{2}-(p-t_{l+1}+t_{l})^{2}\}\alpha_{2}(q),

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t_{l}\leq t,t^{\prime}\leq T\\
\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1\end{subarray}}E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\{(p+t_{l+1}-t_{l})^{2}-2p^{2}+(p-t_{l+1}+t_{l})^{2}\}\beta_{1}(z)+\{p^{2}-(p-t_{l+1}+t_{l})^{2}\}\beta_{2}(z).

(2.3) When
t_{l}-t_{l-1}<p
and
t_{l+1}-t_{l}\geq p
, for
t_{l}\leq\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l-1}+p-1
and
t_{l}\leq\max(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l-1}+p-1
, we have
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=3
; for
t_{l}\leq\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l}+p-1
and
t_{l-1}+p\leq\max(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l}+p-1
, we have
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=2
; for
t_{l}\leq\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1
and
t_{l}+p\leq\max(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}+p-1
, we have
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=1
; for
t_{l}\leq\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1
and
t_{l+1}+p\leq\max(t,t^{\prime})\leq T
, we have
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=0
. In these cases, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t_{l}\leq t,t^{\prime}\leq T\\
\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1\end{subarray}}E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\{(p+t_{l+1}-t_{l})^{2}-2p^{2}\}\alpha_{1}(q)+\{p^{2}-(p-t_{l}+t_{l-1})^{2}\}\alpha_{2}(q)+(p-t_{l}+t_{l-1})^{2}\alpha_{3}(q),

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t_{l}\leq t,t^{\prime}\leq T\\
\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1\end{subarray}}E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\{(p+t_{l+1}-t_{l})^{2}-2p^{2}\}\beta_{1}(z)+\{p^{2}-(p-t_{l}+t_{l-1})^{2}\}\beta_{2}(z)+(p-t_{l}+t_{l-1})^{2}\beta_{3}(z).

(2.4) When
t_{l}-t_{l-1}<p
and
t_{l+1}-t_{l}<p
, for
t_{l}\leq\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l-1}+p-1
and
t_{l}\leq\max(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l-1}+p-1
, we have
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=3
; for
t_{l}\leq\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1
and
t_{l-1}+p\leq\max(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l}+p-1
, we have
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=2
; for
t_{l}\leq\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1
and
t_{l}+p\leq\max(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}+p-1
, we have
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=1
; for
t_{l}\leq\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1
and
t_{l+1}-p\leq\max(t,t^{\prime})\leq T
, we have
J_{t,t^{\prime}}^{\circ}=0
. In these cases, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t_{l}\leq t,t^{\prime}\leq T\\
\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1\end{subarray}}E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}=\{(p+t_{l+1}-t_{l})^{2}-2p^{2}+(p-t_{l+1}+t_{l})^{2}\}\alpha_{1}(q)

\displaystyle+\{p^{2}-(p-t_{l}+t_{l-1})^{2}-(p-t_{l+1}+t_{l})^{2}\}\alpha_{2}(q)+(p-t_{l}+t_{l-1})^{2}\alpha_{3}(q),

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t_{l}\leq t,t^{\prime}\leq T\\
\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1\end{subarray}}E\{\mathbf{S}_{t}(\mathbb{T},z)\mathbf{S}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},z)\}=\{(p+t_{l+1}-t_{l})^{2}-2p^{2}+(p-t_{l+1}+t_{l})^{2}\}\beta_{1}(z)

\displaystyle+\{p^{2}-(p-t_{l}+t_{l-1})^{2}-(p-t_{l+1}+t_{l})^{2}\}\beta_{2}(z)+(p-t_{l}+t_{l-1})^{2}\beta_{3}(z).

Under these four sub-circumstances (2.1)–(2.4), we have, for any
l=1,\ldots,L-1
,

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t_{l}\leq t,t^{\prime}\leq T\\
\min(t,t^{\prime})\leq t_{l+1}-1\end{subarray}}E\{\mathbf{D}_{t}(\mathbb{T},q)\mathbf{D}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathbb{T},q)\}=[(p+t_{l+1}-t_{l})^{2}-2p^{2}+\{(p-t_{l+1}+t_{l})^{+}\}^{2}]\alpha_{1}(q)

\displaystyle+[p^{2}-\{(p-t_{l}+t_{l-1})^{+}\}^{2}-\{(p-t_{l+1}+t_{l})^{+}\}^{2}]\alpha_{2}(q)+\{(p-t_{l}+t_{l-1})^{+}\}^{2}\alpha_{3}(q),

and

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t_{l}\leq t,t^{\prime}\leq T\\
